# Zoho Books REST API v3 operation catalog

> **Readiness: CLOSED for the 2026-08-31 snapshot.** The dated official OpenAPI archive contains 849 operation records, and the official v3 navigation index contains 849 non-Overview operation entries for the same 42 resources (848 unique resource/fragment identities because one Contacts anchor occurs twice). All entries reconcile: 823 by normalized summary/anchor identity and 26 by recorded title/anchor aliases. No operation is silently omitted. This proves snapshot closure, not future or tenant-wide availability.

Related recognition layers: [42 standard resource groups](zoho-books-standard-resources.md) and the [complete published schema/API-field inventory](zoho-books-standard-fields.md). Those catalogs remain recognition-only; this operation catalog remains the per-operation contract.

## Snapshot and closure

| Evidence | Result |
|---|---|
| OpenAPI source | `https://www.zoho.com/books/api/v3/openapi-all.zip` captured 2026-08-31; SHA-256 `c6a841bbc81ef882c64b1f2ad4761e350faefba1db7fb36a32daf7112b647559` |
| Official index | `https://www.zoho.com/books/api/v3/` captured 2026-08-31; downloaded HTML SHA-256 `ffe642b5172332538a2d5d93f3a0aacf893b1a636f200c609bfaece115b40802` |
| Extracted specifications | 42 YAML files; OpenAPI 3.0.0 |
| OpenAPI operation records | 849: GET 263, POST 338, PUT 131, DELETE 117 |
| Index operation entries | 849 non-Overview entries (848 unique resource/fragment identities) across 42 OpenAPI resources |
| Reconciliation | 823 direct normalized matches + 26 aliases = 849; 0 unmatched |
| Stable identity defects | 6 duplicated `operationId` values (12 rows) and 4 duplicated method/path identities (8 rows); preserved and flagged per row |
| Exclusions | Introduction, OAuth, HTTP Methods, Response, Errors, Pagination, and every `Overview` entry are documentation topics, not REST operations. No OpenAPI operation is excluded. |

### Deterministic extraction method

The capture used `curl --fail --location --silent --show-error`, `sha256sum`, and `unzip`. A Python 3 extractor loaded every `*.yml` with PyYAML, enumerated only HTTP method keys under `paths`, resolved parameter references for requiredness, retained schema `$ref` values, and read index links with the standard-library HTML parser. Reconciliation keyed by resource plus normalized summary/fragment; the 26 punctuation, possessive, conjunction, or wording aliases were then paired one-to-one within the same resource using deterministic string similarity with a 0.70 minimum and inspected as a closed set. Temporary downloads and extracted YAML remain outside the repository and are not a maintenance feature.

### Catalog reading rules

- Paths are exactly as published in each OpenAPI file; combine them with the official v3 server/domain rules at runtime.
- Required parameters include required path, query, header, and cookie parameters. `organization_id` is reported independently per operation.
- Schema references preserve auditability without copying large inline definitions. `inline OpenAPI schema` means the snapshot provides an inline shape rather than a reusable `$ref`.
- OAuth scopes are copied from each operation's OpenAPI `security` declaration. `OpenAPI-confirmed` does not certify that a plan, region, role, gateway, or live organization enables the operation.
- Runtime organization metadata and settings are authoritative. New REST code uses v3. Legacy calls are never migrated implicitly: warn and ask migrate-or-preserve; if migration is not approved, retain the existing version in that function.
- Generation may include destructive operations when explicitly requested. Future live destructive or financial execution requires an impact warning and explicit confirmation.
- Use named OAuth connections; the target-oriented default is `conpas_books`. Never request or embed secrets.

## Reconciliation defects and aliases

The defects below do not remove rows; they prevent treating `operationId` or method/path alone as globally unique. Use `(source file/resource, method, path, operationId)` as the snapshot identity.

### Duplicate operation IDs

| Operation ID | Resources |
|---|---|
| `create_contact_person` | `contact-persons`, `contacts` |
| `delete_contact_person` | `contact-persons`, `contacts` |
| `get_contact_person` | `contact-persons`, `contacts` |
| `list_contact_persons` | `contact-persons`, `contacts` |
| `mark_contact_person_primary` | `contact-persons`, `contacts` |
| `update_contact_person` | `contact-persons`, `contacts` |

### Duplicate method/path identities

| Method/path | Operations |
|---|---|
| `GET /contacts/{contact_id}/contactpersons` | `contact-persons:list_contact_persons`, `contacts:list_contact_persons` |
| `GET /invoices` | `customer-debit-notes:list_customer_debit_notes`, `invoices:list_invoices` |
| `POST /contacts/contactpersons` | `contact-persons:create_contact_person`, `contacts:create_contact_person` |
| `POST /invoices` | `customer-debit-notes:create_customer_debit_note`, `invoices:create_invoice` |
